Abacus of Violent Odds, 3, DrHuPaRoShWa
(Outland-208E)
Equipment—Item, Trinket (2)
When your hero attacks, roll a die. If it's even,
remove your hero from combat. If it's odd, ready
your hero and all weapons you control at the
end of this combat.
